- **9Now** (F) — Free ad-funded live/catch-up; Australian access. [Source 1](https://help.9now.com.au/hc/en-au/articles/33270561854489-Is-9Now-free-to-use)
- **10** (F) — Former 10 play; official help confirms free live/catch-up with advertising. [Source 1](https://helpdesk.tenplay.com.au/support/solutions/articles/16000125615-why-does-10-play-have-ads-) [Source 2](https://helpdesk.tenplay.com.au/support/solutions/articles/16000048101-about-10)
- **SBS On Demand** (F) — Free ad-funded service; multilingual VOD/live. Certain ad-category opt-outs are not universal ad-free viewing. [Source 1](https://help.sbs.com.au/hc/en-au/articles/360002314515-Why-is-there-advertising-on-SBS)
- **TVNZ+** (F) — Official media kit confirms free service and video ad breaks. [Source 1](https://sales.tvnz.co.nz/assets/Presentations/TVNZ%2B-Digital-Media-Kit-May-2025.pdf)
- **ThreeNow** (F) — Official developer lists free live/VOD with ads; desktop ad playback still untested. [Source 1](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?hl=en&id=com.mediaworks.android)
- **ViX** (M) — Separate Gratis and paid Premium with/without ads; do not conflate plans. - **Mercado Play** (F) — Free viewing and video advertising documented; use the appropriate local Mercado Libre host. - **Globoplay** (M) — Free tier plus paid with-ads/Premium; pause ads separate from video breaks. [Source 1](https://ajuda.globo.com/article/Quais-os-tipos-de-plano-do-Globoplay) [Source 2](https://globoads.globo.com/para-o-seu-negocio/anunciar-no-globoplay/pause-ads)
- **Shahid** (M) — Ad-funded and subscription models documented; live and VIP distinctions. [Source 1](https://shahid.mbc.net/en/terms-and-conditions) [Source 2](https://www.mbc.net/ar/news/42)
- **eVOD** (F) — Free ad-supported South African service; web runtime still needs qualification. [Source 1](https://www.etv.co.za/press-releases/evod-now-available-whale-tv)
- **AfroLandTV** (M) — Free viewing plus ad-free Premium documented. [Source 1](https://www.afrolandtv.com/upgrade)

- **YouTube Music** (M) — Official free ad-supported music/podcasts; separate from youtube.com adapter. [Source 1](https://support.google.com/youtubemusic/answer/6313529?hl=en)
- **SoundCloud** (M) — Free listening with audio/display ads; Go differs by territory and catalog. [Source 1](https://soundcloud.com/company/plans/go) [Source 2](https://help.soundcloud.com/hc/en-us/articles/31353227038747-Soundcloud-Go-General-FAQs)
- **Amazon Music Free** (F) — Amazon confirms audio ad inventory; distinguish Free from Prime and Unlimited. [Source 1](https://advertising.amazon.com/help/G9T72834T7Z2K2RB)
- **Pandora** (M) — Free ad-supported tier; primarily US service; paid tiers have exceptions. [Source 1](https://alexa.pandora.com/privacy)
- **iHeartRadio** (M) — Official help confirms free version funded by ads; some stations retain commercials for subscribers. [Source 1](https://help.iheart.com/hc/en-us/articles/16944556140685-Why-am-I-hearing-commercials-even-though-I-have-a-subscription)
- **Audacy** (F) — Free stations funded by ads; geographically targeted audio ads documented. - **Audiomack** (M) — Paid upgrade removes ad interruptions; app-only features do not prove browser parity. [Source 1](https://creators.audiomack.com/premium)

- **JioSaavn** (M) — Free listening with Pro ad-free upgrade; browser eligibility/ad format needs runtime check. [Source 1](https://www.jio.com/help/faq/jio-apps/jiosaavn/do-i-have-to-pay-for-using-features-in-jiosaavn/) [Source 2](https://www.jio.com/apps/jiosaavn/)

- **Anghami** (M) — Official help explicitly documents ads on the free plan through a temporary ad-removal offer. - **Pornhub** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed by official ad network; local eligibility required; no playback tested. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **YouPorn** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed; video/display formats must be distinguished. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **RedTube** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed; no playback tested. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **Tube8** (A) — Official network still lists placement; independently verify active service/browser before adapter. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **xHamster** (A) — Official advertising partner lists pre-roll, popunder and other formats. [Source 1](https://trafficstars.com/product/xhamster-ads)
- **Eporner** (A) — Ad-network case study confirms advertising inventory; current player behavior untested. [Source 1](https://trafficstars.com/blog/case-study-scaling-an-igaming-offer-in-mena-to-1536-ftd)
- **DAZN** (M) — Free selection plus paid passes; ad sales documented. Qualify each territory and rights package. [Source 1](https://www.dazn.com/en-IT/help/articles/16738769256733-free-offer) [Source 2](https://biz.dazn.com/mediaguide/)
- **12+ / mako** (M) — Israeli browser video with paid ad-removal/pre-roll-only choices documented; entitlement/territory restrictions apply. [Source 1](https://www.mako.co.il/mako-vod-help/Article-d09b3267d356081026.htm?Partner=blockscomp) [Source 2](https://www.mako.co.il/mako-vod-help/Article-dd0b75e6b096e71026.htm?partner=purchase_page)

## Additional sports surfaces

- **YouTube** (M) — Free ad-supported movie catalog documented in selected countries; creator videos, Shorts and live require separate tests. Existing adapter is not proof of all surfaces. [Source 1](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/16618963?hl=en)
- **Twitch** (M) — Live ads and streamer sponsorships differ; Turbo does not remove every promotion. [Source 1](https://help.twitch.tv/s/article/twitch-turbo-guide?language=en_US)
- **Dailymotion** (F) — In-stream ads; embedded Dailymotion players are another important surface. [Source 1](https://faq.dailymotion.com/hc/en-us/articles/115009132048-Ad-positions)
- **Kick** (F) — Official July 2026 help confirms ads; varies by viewer location/channel/language. [Source 1](https://help.kick.com/en/articles/15300357-advertising-on-kick-for-viewers)
- **Rumble** (M) — Advertising confirmed; free/premium playback and each ad format need testing. [Source 1](https://ads.rumble.com/)
- **Tubi** (F) — Free VOD and live channels; territory-specific catalogs and devices. [Source 1](https://tubitv.com/welcome) [Source 2](https://tubitv.com/help-center/About-Tubi/articles/4409969055259)
- **Pluto TV** (F) — Free live channels and on-demand; not globally available. [Source 1](https://pluto.tv/where-to-watch)
- **The Roku Channel** (M) — Free ad-supported catalog plus subscriptions. Roku documents web in US/Canada/UK; Mexico device-only in cited help. [Source 1](https://support.roku.com/en-gb/article/get-the-roku-channel) [Source 2](https://advertising.roku.com/solutions/advertise/the-roku-channel)
- **Plex** (F) — Target Plex's free catalog, not the user's personal media library. [Source 1](https://support.plex.tv/articles/frequently-asked-questions-vod/)
- **Xumo Play** (F) — Free ad-supported; distinguish Xumo Play from Xumo hardware. [Source 1](https://support.xumo.com/hc/en-us/articles/22542847894804-Do-I-have-to-pay-for-Xumo-Play)
- **Sling Freestream** (F) — Free live/VOD catalog; paid Sling is separate entitlement. [Source 1](https://www.sling.com/help/en/learn-about-sling/promotions/freestream)
- **Philo Free Channels** (F) — Free channels alongside paid Philo plans. [Source 1](https://help.philo.com/account-billing/free-channels-experience/)
- **Prime Video** (M) — Paid-with-ads and a distinct free catalog in supported markets; former Freevee is integrated here. [Source 1](https://advertising.amazon.com/en-us/library/guides/free-ad-supported-streaming-tv/) [Source 2](https://www.itv.com/presscentre/presscentre/media-releases/itvx-premium-launches-prime-video-uk-add-subscription)
- **Netflix** (P) — Paid ad-supported plans; no general free tier. Ads cannot be skipped using the normal player. [Source 1](https://help.netflix.com/go/ads)
- **Disney+** (P) — Paid ad-supported tiers only in relevant markets. [Source 1](https://press.disneyplus.com/ad-supported-disney-plus-plan-now-available-in-the-us) [Source 2](https://www.disneyplus.com/explore/what-is-disneyplus?msockid=3cc1646b361760fe1c2472e0372861a0)
- **Hulu** (P) — Paid ad-supported service; Hulu content inside Disney+ is a separate player surface. [Source 1](https://www.disneyplus.com/explore/what-is-disneyplus?msockid=3cc1646b361760fe1c2472e0372861a0)
- **HBO Max** (P) — Basic with Ads; country-specific plan and ad load. [Source 1](https://help.hbomax.com/US/Answer/Detail/000002520)
- **Paramount+** (P) — Paid ad-supported plans; live TV can retain ads on higher plans. [Source 1](https://www.paramountplus.com/account/signup/pickplan/?msockid=339152c80beb6ac234d844180ada6bab)
- **Peacock** (P) — Current paid Select/Premium options; do not assume a generally available free tier. [Source 1](https://www.peacocktv.com/help/article/why-am-i-seeing-ads-as-a-peacock-premium-plus-customer) [Source 2](https://www.peacocktv.com/plans/all-monthly?successRedirect=%2Fwatch%2Fasset%2Fsports%2Fwwe%2Fwrestlings-bloopers-bleeps-and-bodyslams%2F34153781-8fbf-3c5a-bb5b-3c6ca976ae2d)
- **AMC+** (P) — Paid with-ads option; bundled channel content must retain its access rules. [Source 1](https://join.amcplus.com/home/)
- **discovery+** (P) — Paid ad-supported plan; regional plan availability needs checking. [Source 1](https://help.philo.com/account-billing/packages/)
- **Fandango at Home** (M) — Official catalog explicitly labels free-with-ads titles alongside rentals/purchases. Former Vudu; title availability and desktop playback remain untested. [Source 1](https://athome.fandango.com/content/movies/) [Source 2](https://athome.fandango.com/content/browse/uxrow/Browse-All-Free-Titles/2764) [Source 3](https://athome.fandango.com/content/browse/uxrow/Watch-For-Free-with-Ads-Buy-For-4-99/18120)
- **FilmRise** (F) — Ad-supported apps/channels confirmed; main corporate site is not necessarily a full web player. Test distributor hosts. [Source 1](https://www.filmrise.com/about-us)
- **Fawesome** (F) — Official distributor page documents free, advertising-funded VOD. Its device list emphasizes CTV/mobile; do not assume a usable desktop player. [Source 1](https://fawesome.tv/partnerships/)
- **Filmzie** (F) — Free advertising-funded VOD documented in privacy notice. [Source 1](https://filmzie.com/legal/en/privacy-notice)
- **DistroTV** (F) — FAST and VOD; territory/channel variations. [Source 1](https://blog.distro.tv/news/distrotv-now-set-to-stream-on-major-smart-tv-platforms-in-addition-to-all-key-streaming-devices/)
- **Local Now** (F) — Owner documents a free ad-supported service with local news and entertainment. Current desktop playback and geographic access still need qualification. [Source 1](https://allenmedia.tv/founder/) [Source 2](https://allenmedia.tv/allen-media-group-turns-local-now-into-a-free-hyperlocal-streamer/)
- **Crunchyroll Channel** (F) — Free linear channel on partner platforms; do not treat paid Crunchyroll app as the same service. [Source 1](https://help.crunchyroll.com/article/crunchyroll-channel)
- **Rakuten Viki** (M) — Free with ads plus Viki Pass; catalog varies by territory. [Source 1](https://global.rakuten.com/corp/news/press/2024/1118_01.html)
- **OnDemandKorea** (M) — Official pages document free episodes and advertising on free content; paid plans can reduce ads rather than remove every ad. Qualify free VOD separately from sports and premium titles. [Source 1](https://www.ondemandkorea.com/en/search) [Source 2](https://www.contentpromo.ondemandkorea.com/standard-en)
- **CBC Gem** (M) — CBC advertising sales page confirms no-cost viewing and paid ad-free videos, with Web/App audience reporting. Canadian availability; live and pause ads require separate qualification. [Source 1](https://solutionsmedia.cbcrc.ca/en/platforms/cbc-gem) [Source 2](https://apps.apple.com/ca/app/cbc-gem-movies-tv-live/id422191503?ls=1)
- **Crave** (P) — Current Standard With Ads documented by Bell; do not assume all Crave is free. [Source 1](https://www.bell.ca/mobility/promotions/Crave-plans)
- **ITVX** (M) — Free ads and Premium; live channels still have ads. [Source 1](https://help.itv.com/support/solutions/articles/204000073725-why-does-itvx-have-ads-) [Source 2](https://help.itv.com/support/solutions/articles/204000074053/create_ticket)
- **Channel 4** (M) — Former All 4; free streaming with optional Channel 4+ ad-free offering. [Source 1](https://www.channel4.com/press/news/channel-4-brings-iconic-blocks-back-together-single-brand-streaming-future) [Source 2](https://assets-corporate.channel4.com/_flysystem/s3/2024-01/Fast%20Forward%20-%20Channel%204%20strategy%20summary.pdf)
- **U** (F) — Former UKTV Play; free ad-funded streaming documented. [Source 1](https://u.co.uk/help/faqs)
- **STV Player** (M) — Free with ads; subscription/live exceptions. [Source 1](https://support.stv.tv/support/subscription-pricing) [Source 2](https://support.stv.tv/support/why-does-stv-player-have-ads-)
- **RTÉ Player** (B) — Live sports sponsorship/ad breaks confirmed; VOD must be tested separately. [Source 1](https://about.rte.ie/2026/06/03/rte-unveils-heineken-0-0-as-broadcast-sponsor-of-fifa-world-cup-2026-coverage/)
- **Virgin Media Play** (F) — Free Irish catch-up/live funded by advertising. [Source 1](https://www.virginmediatelevision.ie/player-faq)
- **NOW** (P) — Paid on-demand includes ads without Boost/Ultra Boost; live-channel ads remain. [Source 1](https://www.nowtv.com/gb/help/article/can-i-watch-without-ads)
- **TF1+** (M) — Free ad-funded catalog; Premium and live exceptions. Former MYTF1. [Source 1](https://help.tf1.fr/hc/fr/articles/16165591640338-Pourquoi-je-ne-peux-pas-acc%C3%A9der-gratuitement-aux-programmes-et-au-direct-de-cha%C3%AEnes-sur-TF1) [Source 2](https://help.tf1.fr/hc/fr/articles/16165603277074-TF1-une-offre-payante-ou-gratuite)
- **M6+** (M) — Free ad-funded with paid options. Former 6play. [Source 1](https://support.m6plus.m6.fr/hc/fr/articles/27304420678300-Pourquoi-je-ne-peux-pas-acc%C3%A9der-gratuitement-aux-programmes-et-au-direct-de-cha%C3%AEnes-sur-M6) [Source 2](https://support.m6plus.m6.fr/hc/fr/articles/13284090995612-Pourquoi-y-a-t-il-des-publicit%C3%A9s-lorsque-je-regarde-un-programme-sur-M6)
- **france.tv** (F) — Official ad-sales site documents free streaming and advertising formats. This establishes an advertising offering, not ads on every program or a skippable segment; qualify children/live/VOD separately. [Source 1](https://www.francetvpub.fr/marques/france-tv/) [Source 2](https://www.francetvpub.fr/?jet_download=33025)
- **Rakuten TV** (M) — Free ad-supported selection plus transactions; website login needed for Free. [Source 1](https://support.rakuten.tv/hc/en-us/articles/360036407474-What-is-Rakuten-TV-Free) [Source 2](https://support.rakuten.tv/hc/en-us/articles/360010835133-Technical-requirements-to-use-Rakuten-TV-on-your-Computer)
- **Samsung TV Plus** (F) — Samsung documents FAST advertising and US web-player access. Service availability in other countries does not prove the same browser access. Live playback untested. [Source 1](https://www.samsung.com/us/tvs/smart-tv/samsung-tv-plus/) [Source 2](https://www.samsung.com/us/business/samsungads/blog/2025-newfront-highlights/)
- **LG Channels** (F) — LG documents free ad-supported channels and US PC-browser access. Global service country counts include device distribution and do not establish browser availability everywhere. [Source 1](https://www.lg.com/us/lg-channels) [Source 2](https://www.lgchannels.com/) [Source 3](https://www.lg.com/global/newsroom/news/media-entertainment-solution/lg-channels-surpasses-5000-channels-globally-as-poland-launch-brings-service-to-37-countries/)
- **wedotv** (F) — Official editorial page describes free advertising-funded movies/series; privacy center documents video and ad events. Current territory and desktop playback remain untested. [Source 1](https://blog.wedotv.com/en/article/what-is-avod) [Source 2](https://wedotv.com/dsa/)
- **SkyShowtime** (P) — Paid Standard with Ads across relevant European markets. [Source 1](https://corporate.skyshowtime.com/234778-skyshowtime-announces-introduction-of-new-standard-with-ads-plan/) [Source 2](https://corporate.skyshowtime.com/242082-skyshowtime-introduces-a-new-premium-subscription-plan/)
- **Joyn Germany** (M) — Free ad-funded service plus paid Joyn+; distinguish live from VOD. [Source 1](https://www.joyn.de/joyn-plus) [Source 2](https://www.joyn.de/bts/themen/service/warum-werbung-19845)
- **Joyn Austria** (F) — Owner explicitly documents Austria free ad-funded live/on-demand service and joyn.at access in May 2026. Separate from the German surface; actual player/ad behavior untested. [Source 1](https://www.prosiebensat1puls4.com/n/sevenone-austria/)
- **RTL+ Germany** (M) — Official terms document Free with advertising; paid plans also differ. [Source 1](https://assets.plus.rtl.de/bouncer/agb.html)
- **Zattoo** (M) — Free ad-funded Germany/Switzerland; channel/device restrictions. [Source 1](https://support.zattoo.com/hc/de/articles/34124449157010-Werbung-auf-Zattoo-mit-und-ohne-Abonnement) [Source 2](https://support.zattoo.com/hc/en-us/articles/34277042164626-Zattoo-prices-and-features-at-a-glance)
- **Mediaset Infinity Italy** (M) — Official GOLD feature reduces advertising interruptions on free content; Infinity+ entitlements separate. [Source 1](https://help.mediasetinfinity.mediaset.it/hc/it/articles/24880779096977-Scopri-la-GOLD-experience)
- **Mediaset Infinity Spain** (F) — Former Mitele; free catalog and digital advertising documented. Validate current redirects/hostname before code. [Source 1](https://www.mediaset.es/comunicacion/corporativo/publiespana/20250624/publiespana-ad-infinity_18_015948269.html)

- **TV4 Play** (M) — Free and paid-with-ads; sport/live can retain commercial breaks. [Source 1](https://www.tv4play.se/var-behandlar-vi-datan) [Source 2](https://www.tv4play.se/paket?return=%2Fkanaler%2Ftabla%3Fd%3D2026-03-11)
- **Ruutu** (M) — Free Pluto catalog and paid Ruutu+ with ads. [Source 1](https://asiakastuki.ruutu.fi/article/152-ruudun-hinnasto) [Source 2](https://www.ruutu.fi/plutotv-sisaltokirjasto)
- **Viaplay** (P) — Paid with-ads packages and live-ad exceptions; verify each country. [Source 1](https://help.viaplay.com/is/advertisements-on-viaplay/)
- **Player Poland** (M) — Free channels and paid ad-free offering; verify ad behavior by package. [Source 1](https://promocja.player.pl/sprawdz-oferte-ogladaj-wiecej) [Source 2](https://www.promocja.player.pl/bez-reklam)
- **Ivi** (M) — Free content interrupted by ads documented; subscription and live exceptions. [Source 1](https://ask.ivi.ru/knowledge-bases/10/articles/41543-reklama-na-sajte-ivi-sdelat-eyo-menshe-ili-sovsem-ubrat) [Source 2](https://www.ivi.ru/pages/agreement/agreement.txt)
- **JioHotstar** (P) — Paid ad-supported Mobile/Super plans; Premium entertainment exception does not remove live sports ads. Some free sampling may vary. - **SonyLIV** (M) — Official FAQ confirms free-user ads and paid ad-supported plans. [Source 1](https://www.sonyliv.com/faqs)
- **ZEE5** (P) — Indian plan table explicitly lists paid plans with ads and Laptop/PC support. This is not evidence of a general free tier. Regional, kids and live plans differ. - **TVING** (P) — Paid advertising-supported tier; TVING/Wavve bundles do not prove unified player. [Source 1](https://www.tving.com/bill/subscription/plan?tab=plus) [Source 2](https://www.tving.com/bill/subscription/plan?returnUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.tving.com%2Flive%2FC51855)
- **LINE TV Taiwan** (M) — Free titles with ads plus VIP; unrelated to closed LINE TV Thailand. [Source 1](https://help.linetv.tw/hc/zh-tw/articles/48925851740441-%E8%A7%80%E7%9C%8B%E5%BD%B1%E7%89%87%E6%98%AF%E5%90%A6%E9%9C%80%E8%A6%81%E4%BB%98%E8%B2%BB)
- **Viu** (M) — Pan-regional service; official developer confirms premium avoids ads. Free catalog and desktop behavior vary by market. [Source 1](https://hq.viu.com/) [Source 2](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.viu.phone)
- **WeTV** (M) — Official free viewing and ad-free VIP described; title/region restrictions. [Source 1](https://wetv.vip/en?appid=coop_direct_msite_EASIMY%2F1000)

- **Audacy** (F) — Free stations funded by ads; geographically targeted audio ads documented. - **LiveOne** (M) — Official help confirms free ad-sponsored service after paid cancellation. [Source 1](https://support.liveone.com/hc/en-us/articles/4415471548315-Cancel-a-LiveOne-Subscription)
- **Audiomack** (M) — Paid upgrade removes ad interruptions; app-only features do not prove browser parity. [Source 1](https://creators.audiomack.com/premium)
- **iVoox** (M) — Free podcasts/radio plus ad-free paid options; host-read sponsorship remains separate. [Source 1](https://www.ivoox.com/planes) [Source 2](https://www.ivoox.com/en/)
- **JioSaavn** (M) — Free listening with Pro ad-free upgrade; browser eligibility/ad format needs runtime check. [Source 1](https://www.jio.com/help/faq/jio-apps/jiosaavn/do-i-have-to-pay-for-using-features-in-jiosaavn/) [Source 2](https://www.jio.com/apps/jiosaavn/)
- **Boomplay** (M) — Official developer describes free service funded by ads; browser feature parity unverified. [Source 1](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?hl=en_US&id=com.afmobi.boomplayer)
- **Anghami** (M) — Official help explicitly documents ads on the free plan through a temporary ad-removal offer. - **TREBEL** (M) — Official app describes free daily allowance with ads; app-first, desktop player not established. [Source 1](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?hl=en_US&id=com.mmm.trebelmusic)
- **Pornhub** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed by official ad network; local eligibility required; no playback tested. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **YouPorn** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed; video/display formats must be distinguished. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **RedTube** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed; no playback tested. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **Tube8** (A) — Official network still lists placement; independently verify active service/browser before adapter. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **xHamster** (A) — Official advertising partner lists pre-roll, popunder and other formats. [Source 1](https://trafficstars.com/product/xhamster-ads)
- **Eporner** (A) — Ad-network case study confirms advertising inventory; current player behavior untested. [Source 1](https://trafficstars.com/blog/case-study-scaling-an-igaming-offer-in-mena-to-1536-ftd)
- **DAZN** (M) — Free selection plus paid passes; ad sales documented. Qualify each territory and rights package. [Source 1](https://www.dazn.com/en-IT/help/articles/16738769256733-free-offer) [Source 2](https://biz.dazn.com/mediaguide/)
- **12+ / mako** (M) — Israeli browser video with paid ad-removal/pre-roll-only choices documented; entitlement/territory restrictions apply. ## Iran and Israel

- **YouTube** (M) — Free ad-supported movie catalog documented in selected countries; creator videos, Shorts and live require separate tests. Existing adapter is not proof of all surfaces. [Source 1](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/16618963?hl=en)
- **Twitch** (M) — Live ads and streamer sponsorships differ; Turbo does not remove every promotion. [Source 1](https://help.twitch.tv/s/article/twitch-turbo-guide?language=en_US)

- **Rumble** (M) — Advertising confirmed; free/premium playback and each ad format need testing. [Source 1](https://ads.rumble.com/)
- **Tubi** (F) — Free VOD and live channels; territory-specific catalogs and devices. [Source 1](https://tubitv.com/welcome) [Source 2](https://tubitv.com/help-center/About-Tubi/articles/4409969055259)
- **Pluto TV** (F) — Free live channels and on-demand; not globally available. [Source 1](https://pluto.tv/where-to-watch)
- **The Roku Channel** (M) — Free ad-supported catalog plus subscriptions. Roku documents web in US/Canada/UK; Mexico device-only in cited help. [Source 1](https://support.roku.com/en-gb/article/get-the-roku-channel) [Source 2](https://advertising.roku.com/solutions/advertise/the-roku-channel)

- **Xumo Play** (F) — Free ad-supported; distinguish Xumo Play from Xumo hardware. [Source 1](https://support.xumo.com/hc/en-us/articles/22542847894804-Do-I-have-to-pay-for-Xumo-Play)
- **Sling Freestream** (F) — Free live/VOD catalog; paid Sling is separate entitlement. [Source 1](https://www.sling.com/help/en/learn-about-sling/promotions/freestream)
- **Philo Free Channels** (F) — Free channels alongside paid Philo plans. [Source 1](https://help.philo.com/account-billing/free-channels-experience/)

- **Paramount+** (P) — Paid ad-supported plans; live TV can retain ads on higher plans. [Source 1](https://www.paramountplus.com/account/signup/pickplan/?msockid=339152c80beb6ac234d844180ada6bab)

- **9Now** (F) — Free ad-funded live/catch-up; Australian access. [Source 1](https://help.9now.com.au/hc/en-au/articles/33270561854489-Is-9Now-free-to-use)
- **10** (F) — Former 10 play; official help confirms free live/catch-up with advertising. [Source 1](https://helpdesk.tenplay.com.au/support/solutions/articles/16000125615-why-does-10-play-have-ads-) [Source 2](https://helpdesk.tenplay.com.au/support/solutions/articles/16000048101-about-10)
- **SBS On Demand** (F) — Free ad-funded service; multilingual VOD/live. Certain ad-category opt-outs are not universal ad-free viewing. [Source 1](https://help.sbs.com.au/hc/en-au/articles/360002314515-Why-is-there-advertising-on-SBS)
- **TVNZ+** (F) — Official media kit confirms free service and video ad breaks. [Source 1](https://sales.tvnz.co.nz/assets/Presentations/TVNZ%2B-Digital-Media-Kit-May-2025.pdf)
- **ThreeNow** (F) — Official developer lists free live/VOD with ads; desktop ad playback still untested. [Source 1](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?hl=en&id=com.mediaworks.android)
- **ViX** (M) — Separate Gratis and paid Premium with/without ads; do not conflate plans. [Source 1](https://ayuda.vix.com/hc/es/articles/26019677892365-Preguntas-frecuentes-sobre-ViX-Con-Anuncios) [Source 2](https://ayuda.vix.com/hc/es/articles/42642211728781--Apostar%C3%ADas-por-M%C3%AD-Puedo-probar-el-contenido-Premium-en-ViX-sin-suscribirme)
- **Canela.TV** (F) — Official FAQ: free with ads, web available; US and much of Latin America. [Source 1](https://static-assets-cdn.edge.api.canela.tv/static/config/static-config/faq.html)
- **Mercado Play** (F) — Free viewing and video advertising documented; use the appropriate local Mercado Libre host. [Source 1](https://global-selling.mercadolibre.com/learning-center/news/find-out?suScope=prod) [Source 2](https://investor.mercadolibre.com/sites/mercadolibre/files/mercadolibre/results/mercadoLibre-Q424-sec-show.pdf)
- **Globoplay** (M) — Free tier plus paid with-ads/Premium; pause ads separate from video breaks. [Source 1](https://ajuda.globo.com/article/Quais-os-tipos-de-plano-do-Globoplay) [Source 2](https://globoads.globo.com/para-o-seu-negocio/anunciar-no-globoplay/pause-ads)
- **Shahid** (M) — Ad-funded and subscription models documented; live and VIP distinctions. [Source 1](https://shahid.mbc.net/en/terms-and-conditions) [Source 2](https://www.mbc.net/ar/news/42)
- **eVOD** (F) — Free ad-supported South African service; web runtime still needs qualification. [Source 1](https://www.etv.co.za/press-releases/evod-now-available-whale-tv)
- **AfroLandTV** (M) — Free viewing plus ad-free Premium documented. [Source 1](https://www.afrolandtv.com/upgrade)
- **Spotify** (M) — Free songs/podcasts with ads; music breaks and podcast sponsorships are separate problems. [Source 1](https://open.spotify.com/browse/albums)
- **YouTube Music** (M) — Official free ad-supported music/podcasts; separate from youtube.com adapter. [Source 1](https://support.google.com/youtubemusic/answer/6313529?hl=en)
- **SoundCloud** (M) — Free listening with audio/display ads; Go differs by territory and catalog. [Source 1](https://soundcloud.com/company/plans/go) [Source 2](https://help.soundcloud.com/hc/en-us/articles/31353227038747-Soundcloud-Go-General-FAQs)
- **Amazon Music Free** (F) — Amazon confirms audio ad inventory; distinguish Free from Prime and Unlimited. [Source 1](https://advertising.amazon.com/help/G9T72834T7Z2K2RB)
- **Pandora** (M) — Free ad-supported tier; primarily US service; paid tiers have exceptions. [Source 1](https://alexa.pandora.com/privacy)
- **iHeartRadio** (M) — Official help confirms free version funded by ads; some stations retain commercials for subscribers. [Source 1](https://help.iheart.com/hc/en-us/articles/16944556140685-Why-am-I-hearing-commercials-even-though-I-have-a-subscription)
- **Audacy** (F) — Free stations funded by ads; geographically targeted audio ads documented. [Source 1](https://support.audacy.com/hc/en-us/articles/13767116748059-Is-Audacy-Free) [Source 2](https://support.audacy.com/hc/en-us/articles/39469742083995-Why-am-I-hearing-ads-for-a-different-area-or-city-than-my-current-location)
- **LiveOne** (M) — Official help confirms free ad-sponsored service after paid cancellation. [Source 1](https://support.liveone.com/hc/en-us/articles/4415471548315-Cancel-a-LiveOne-Subscription)
- **Audiomack** (M) — Paid upgrade removes ad interruptions; app-only features do not prove browser parity. [Source 1](https://creators.audiomack.com/premium)
- **iVoox** (M) — Free podcasts/radio plus ad-free paid options; host-read sponsorship remains separate. [Source 1](https://www.ivoox.com/planes) [Source 2](https://www.ivoox.com/en/)
- **JioSaavn** (M) — Free listening with Pro ad-free upgrade; browser eligibility/ad format needs runtime check. [Source 1](https://www.jio.com/help/faq/jio-apps/jiosaavn/do-i-have-to-pay-for-using-features-in-jiosaavn/) [Source 2](https://www.jio.com/apps/jiosaavn/)
- **Boomplay** (M) — Official developer describes free service funded by ads; browser feature parity unverified. [Source 1](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?hl=en_US&id=com.afmobi.boomplayer)
- **Anghami** (M) — Official help explicitly documents ads on the free plan through a temporary ad-removal offer. Homepage plan extraction remains ambiguous; territory eligibility and desktop parity are untested. [Source 1](https://support.anghami.com/hc/en-us/articles/9198311576602-Anghami-Ads-Free-Offer-How-It-Works) [Source 2](https://support.anghami.com/hc/en-us/categories/204110868-Advertise-on-Anghami) [Source 3](https://support.anghami.com/hc/en-us/articles/9198311576602-Anghami-Ads-Free-Offer-How-It-Works)
- **TREBEL** (M) — Official app describes free daily allowance with ads; app-first, desktop player not established. [Source 1](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?hl=en_US&id=com.mmm.trebelmusic)
- **Pornhub** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed by official ad network; local eligibility required; no playback tested. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **YouPorn** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed; video/display formats must be distinguished. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **RedTube** (A) — Adult ad placement confirmed; no playback tested. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **Tube8** (A) — Official network still lists placement; independently verify active service/browser before adapter. [Source 1](https://www.trafficjunky.com/advertiser/ad-formats)
- **xHamster** (A) — Official advertising partner lists pre-roll, popunder and other formats. [Source 1](https://trafficstars.com/product/xhamster-ads)
- **Eporner** (A) — Ad-network case study confirms advertising inventory; current player behavior untested. [Source 1](https://trafficstars.com/blog/case-study-scaling-an-igaming-offer-in-mena-to-1536-ftd)
- **DAZN** (M) — Free selection plus paid passes; ad sales documented. Qualify each territory and rights package. [Source 1](https://www.dazn.com/en-IT/help/articles/16738769256733-free-offer) [Source 2](https://biz.dazn.com/mediaguide/)
- **12+ / mako** (M) — Israeli browser video with paid ad-removal/pre-roll-only choices documented; entitlement/territory restrictions apply. [Source 1](https://www.mako.co.il/mako-vod-help/Article-d09b3267d356081026.htm?Partner=blockscomp) [Source 2](https://www.mako.co.il/mako-vod-help/Article-dd0b75e6b096e71026.htm?partner=purchase_page)
